```ts
  });

  it("setting revoked_at writes a catalogue tombstone so a disconnected till learns of it (test 34)", async () => {
    const e = await seedEmployee(db, a, { employeeNumber: "E-REV" });
    const p = await seedCashier(db, a, { employeeId: e, branchId: a.hq });
    await withTenant(db.rw, a.tenant, a.human, (tx) =>
      tx`update app.pos_cashier set revoked_at = now() where principal_id = ${p}`);
    const tombs = await withTenant(db.rw, a.tenant, a.human, (tx) => tx<
      { stream: string; row_id: string; branch_id: string | null }[]
    >`select stream, row_id, branch_id from app.catalogue_tombstone where row_id = ${p}`);
    expect(tombs).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(tombs[0]).toMatchObject({ stream: "cashier", row_id: p, branch_id: a.hq });
  });

  it("re-revoking writes no second tombstone — the trigger fires on the transition only", async () => {
    const e = await seedEmployee(db, a, { employeeNumber: "E-REV2" });
    const p = await seedCashier(db, a, { employeeId: e, branchId: a.hq });
    await withTenant(db.rw, a.tenant, a.human, async (tx) => {
      await tx`update app.pos_cashier set revoked_at = now() where principal_id = ${p}`;
      await tx`update app.pos_cashier set revoked_at = now() where principal_id = ${p}`;
    });
    const [{ n }] = await withTenant(db.rw, a.tenant, a.human, (tx) =>
      tx<{ n: number }[]>`select count(*)::int as n from app.catalogue_tombstone where row_id = ${p}`);
    expect(n).toBe(1);
  });

  it("reinstating and revoking again UPDATES the tombstone rather than aborting on its unique key", async () => {
    // app.catalogue_tombstone carries `unique (stream, row_id)` (6A's 0059) and 6A's own trigger upserts
    // for that reason. Reinstatement is the only way back for a cashier — deletion is not admitted
    // (spec:2049) — so revoke -> un-revoke -> revoke is a real sequence, and a bare insert aborts it.
    const e = await seedEmployee(db, a, { employeeNumber: "E-REV3" });
    const p = await seedCashier(db, a, { employeeId: e, branchId: a.hq });
    await withTenant(db.rw, a.tenant, a.human, (tx) =>
      tx`update app.pos_cashier set revoked_at = now() where principal_id = ${p}`);
    const [first] = await withTenant(db.rw, a.tenant, a.human, (tx) => tx<{ revision: string }[]>`
      select revision::text as revision from app.catalogue_tombstone where row_id = ${p}`);
    await withTenant(db.rw, a.tenant, a.human, (tx) =>
      tx`update app.pos_cashier set revoked_at = null where principal_id = ${p}`);
    await withTenant(db.rw, a.tenant, a.human, (tx) =>
      tx`update app.pos_cashier set revoked_at = now() where principal_id = ${p}`);
    const tombs = await withTenant(db.rw, a.tenant, a.human, (tx) => tx<{ revision: string }[]>`
      select revision::text as revision from app.catalogue_tombstone where row_id = ${p}`);
    expect(tombs).toHaveLength(1);                                     // still ONE row
    expect(BigInt(tombs[0]!.revision)).toBeGreaterThan(BigInt(first!.revision));   // carrying the NEW revision
  });

  it("app.pos_cashier_sync is the down-sync stream 6A's readCatalogue calls, and it hides revoked rows", async () => {
    const e = await seedEmployee(db, a, { employeeNumber: "E-SYNC" });
    const p = await seedCashier(db, a, { employeeId: e, branchId: a.hq });
    const live = await withTenant(db.rw, a.tenant, a.human, (tx) => tx<
      { id: string; revision: string; row: { pin_hash: string; tenant_id?: string } }[]
    >`select id::text as id, revision::text as revision, row from app.pos_cashier_sync(${a.company}, ${a.hq}, 0)`);
    const mine = live.find((r) => r.id === p);
    expect(mine).toBeDefined();
    expect(mine!.row.pin_hash).toMatch(/^\$argon2id\$/);   // the till verifies the PIN against this mirror
    expect(mine!.row.tenant_id).toBeUndefined();           // stripped, exactly as 6A strips it on every stream
    await withTenant(db.rw, a.tenant, a.human, (tx) =>
      tx`update app.pos_cashier set revoked_at = now() where principal_id = ${p}`);
    const after = await withTenant(db.rw, a.tenant, a.human, (tx) => tx<{ id: string }[]>`
      select id::text as id from app.pos_cashier_sync(${a.company}, ${a.hq}, 0)`);
    expect(after.map((r) => r.id)).not.toContain(p);       // it leaves as a TOMBSTONE, never as a live upsert
    // and a cashier at another branch is not in this branch's stream at all
    const other = await withTenant(db.rw, a.tenant, a.human, (tx) => tx<{ id: string }[]>`
      select id::text as id from app.pos_cashier_sync(${a.company}, ${await uuidv7(db.admin)}, 0)`);
    expect(other).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it("tenant B cannot see tenant A's cashiers", async () => {
    const e = await seedEmployee(db, a, { employeeNumber: "E-ISO2" });
    const p = await seedCashier(db, a, { employeeId: e, branchId: a.hq });
    const rows = await withTenant(db.rw, b.tenant, b.human, (tx) =>
      tx`select principal_id from app.pos_cashier where principal_id = ${p}`);
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it("the schema linter reports zero violations after 0065", async () => {
    expect(await lintSchema(db.admin)).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write `0065_create_pos_cashier.sql`**

```sql
-- 0065_create_pos_cashier.sql — the till credential (§4.6.3; spec:1324, 2097; LEGACY-025, 026, 028).
-- The PIN is the ONLY credential this application hashes and it is NEVER a login: no route compares one,
-- and verification happens at the till against the down-synced mirror.

-- ── 1. the table ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
create table app.pos_cashier (
  principal_id     uuid primary key references app.principal(id),   -- keyed on the human principal (spec:2097); no separate id (NO_ID_TABLES)
  tenant_id        uuid not null references app.tenant(id),
  company_id       uuid not null,
  employee_id      uuid not null,
  branch_id        uuid not null,
  pin_hash         text not null,                                    -- Argon2id PHC string, per-cashier salt (spec:1324); written only by the PIN endpoints
  pin_updated_at   timestamptz not null default now(),
  snapshot         jsonb not null default '{}'::jsonb,               -- projection: { "codes": [...], "limits": {...}, "revision": n } — §4.6.3, ADR 0061
  snapshot_at      timestamptz,
  revision         bigint not null default 0,                        -- catalogue revision (§4.10.2)
  revoked_at       timestamptz,
  created_at       timestamptz not null default now(),
  unique (tenant_id, principal_id),
  foreign key (tenant_id, company_id)  references app.company(tenant_id, id),
  foreign key (tenant_id, employee_id) references app.employee(tenant_id, id),
  foreign key (tenant_id, branch_id)   references app.dimension_value(tenant_id, id)
);
comment on column app.pos_cashier.snapshot is
  'A PROJECTION regenerated by app.refresh_pos_cashier (0067). Never edited by hand; never read by the server to make a decision (spec:1324, D14).';

call app_meta.enable_tenant_rls('app.pos_cashier');
-- No attach_audit: AUDIT_EXEMPT (§3.5.3). An audited before/after image of pin_hash is a second copy of a
-- credential sitting in a table with a different read gate, and the snapshot is derived data (LEGACY-028).

-- ── 2. catalogue revision — the row is a down-synced stream (§4.10.2, 6A's trigger) ──────────────────
create trigger catalogue_revision before insert or update on app.pos_cashier
  for each row execute function app.catalogue_revision('cashier');

-- ── 3. revocation is visible to a disconnected till (spec:1324, 2090; test 34) ───────────────────────
-- 6A's generic trigger tombstones an ARCHIVAL; a cashier is REVOKED, and revoked_at is a different column,
-- so the transition gets its own AFTER trigger. It fires on the null -> value transition only, so a second
-- revoke writes no second tombstone and the till's cursor advances once.
-- The `on conflict` is not defensive padding: app.catalogue_tombstone carries `unique (stream, row_id)`
-- (6A's 0059) and 6A's own trigger upserts for the same reason -- "a resurrection is an upsert, not a
-- second death". Reinstating a cashier is `revoked_at = null` followed by a later revoke, and without the
-- clause that second revoke aborts the whole reinstatement transaction on the unique key.
create function app.pos_cashier_tombstone() returns trigger language plpgsql as $$
begin
  insert into app.catalogue_tombstone (tenant_id, company_id, stream, row_id, revision, branch_id)
  values (new.tenant_id, new.company_id, 'cashier', new.principal_id, new.revision, new.branch_id)
  on conflict (stream, row_id) do update set revision = excluded.revision, branch_id = excluded.branch_id;
  return null;
end $$;

create trigger pos_cashier_tombstone after update of revoked_at on app.pos_cashier
  for each row when (old.revoked_at is null and new.revoked_at is not null)
  execute function app.pos_cashier_tombstone();

-- ── 4. the down-sync stream 6A's readCatalogue calls (§4.10.2; 6A:2913) ──────────────────────────────
-- 6A's readCatalogue probes `to_regclass('app.pos_cashier')` and, when it is not null, executes
--   select ... from app.pos_cashier_sync($company, $branch, $since)
-- so this function MUST ship in the same migration as the table, or every GET /v1/sync/catalogue and 6A's
-- own readCatalogue suite fail with "function app.pos_cashier_sync(uuid, uuid, bigint) does not exist" the
-- moment 0065 is applied. 6A fixes the signature: (p_company_id uuid, p_branch_id uuid, p_since bigint)
-- returns table (id uuid, revision bigint, row jsonb). Naming a function rather than the table keeps 6A from
-- hard-coding column names it does not own.
--
-- A REVOKED cashier is excluded, exactly as 6A excludes `archived_at is not null` from every live leg: the
-- revoke bumps the revision AND writes the tombstone at the same revision, so admitting the live row would
-- put an upsert and a delete for one id in one window and the till's apply order would decide by accident.
-- `pin_hash` DOES travel -- the till verifies the PIN offline against this mirror (spec:1324) -- and
-- `tenant_id` does not, matching 6A's `to_jsonb(i) - 'tenant_id'`.
create function app.pos_cashier_sync(p_company_id uuid, p_branch_id uuid, p_since bigint)
returns table (id uuid, revision bigint, row jsonb)
language sql stable as $$
  select c.principal_id, c.revision, to_jsonb(c) - 'tenant_id'
    from app.pos_cashier c
   where c.company_id = p_company_id
     and c.branch_id = p_branch_id
     and c.revision > p_since
     and c.revoked_at is null
$$;
grant execute on function app.pos_cashier_sync(uuid, uuid, bigint) to app_rw;

-- ── 5. lookups the sync apply and the compare job make on every batch ────────────────────────────────
create index pos_cashier_branch_idx on app.pos_cashier (company_id, branch_id) where revoked_at is null;
create index pos_cashier_employee_idx on app.pos_cashier (employee_id);
create index pos_cashier_cursor_idx on app.pos_cashier (company_id, branch_id, revision);
```

`packages/db/migrations/0065.down.sql`:

```sql
drop function if exists app.pos_cashier_sync(uuid, uuid, bigint);
drop trigger if exists pos_cashier_tombstone on app.pos_cashier;
drop function if exists app.pos_cashier_tombstone() cascade;
drop trigger if exists catalogue_revision on app.pos_cashier;
drop table if exists app.pos_cashier;
```

**6A's `app.catalogue_revision()` reads its stream from `tg_argv[0]`** (6A's 0059, section 3), which is why section 2 passes `'cashier'`; Task 0's second informational probe prints that line. If the merged file disagrees, attach the trigger as 6A wrote it and change nothing else — that call is 6A's interface, not this slice's, and inventing a second trigger for the same job is hazard 9. The tombstone trigger in section 3 is 6C's either way: `revoked_at` is a column 6A has never seen, and its `on conflict (stream, row_id) do update` matches 6A's own trigger, because `app.catalogue_tombstone` carries `unique (stream, row_id)` and *"a resurrection is an upsert, not a second death"*.

- [ ] **Step 8: Add `seedCashier` to `packages/db/test/fixtures.ts`**

```ts
/**
 * A cashier: a human principal with a membership, a `pos_cashier` row with a real Argon2id hash, and —
 * when `permissions` is given — a branch-restricted role assignment through 2B's `grantCodes`, which is
 * what makes `app.principal_has(principal, code, company, branch)` answer true (§4.5.3).
 * Returns the principal id, which is the row's primary key.
 */
export async function seedCashier(
  db: Db,
  s: Seed,
  opts: { employeeId: string; branchId: string; pin?: string; permissions?: readonly string[] },
): Promise<string> {
  const principalId = await uuidv7(db.admin);
  const phc = await hashPin(opts.pin ?? "1234");
  await withTenant(db.migrator, s.tenant, s.system, async (tx) => {
    await tx`insert into app.principal (id, kind, auth_subject, display_name)
             values (${principalId}, 'human', ${"sub-cashier-" + principalId}, 'Cashier')`;
    await tx`insert into app.membership (tenant_id, principal_id) values (${s.tenant}, ${principalId})`;
  });
  await withTenant(db.migrator, s.tenant, s.system, (tx) => tx`
    insert into app.pos_cashier (principal_id, tenant_id, company_id, employee_id, branch_id, pin_hash)
    values (${principalId}, ${s.tenant}, ${s.company}, ${opts.employeeId}, ${opts.branchId}, ${phc})`);
  if (opts.permissions && opts.permissions.length > 0) {
    await grantCodes(db, s, { codes: opts.permissions, principalId, roleCode: `cashier-${principalId.slice(-8)}`, branchIds: [opts.branchId] });
  }
  await withTenant(db.migrator, s.tenant, s.system, (tx) => tx`select app.refresh_pos_cashier(${principalId})`);
  return principalId;
}
```

⚠️ **All three statements run as `db.migrator` / `s.system`, and that is amendment A15.** The `app.principal` / `app.membership` pair has to, because `app.principal` is global and `app_rw` cannot write it. The **`pos_cashier` insert** and the **`refresh_pos_cashier` call** have to for a different reason: from Task 5's migration 0068 `app.pos_cashier` carries Form A of the RESTRICTIVE `branch_isolation` family, `app.actor_in_branch` is true only for a `kind = 'system'` principal or one with a live `app.role_assignment` in the company, `seedTenant` grants `s.human` nothing (§4.1, pinned), and a policy declared with `using` and no `with check` is applied AS the `with check` on INSERT. As `s.human` the insert fails with `new row violates row-level security policy for table "pos_cashier"`, and the refresh — an UPDATE, so subject to the same `using` clause — silently updates zero rows and leaves an empty snapshot behind. 6D's `seedShift` is written the same way for the same reason (6D amendment A17).

⚠️ **The last line calls a function Task 7 creates.** Until 0067's companion function exists this helper fails; that is deliberate and is why the `refresh` call is written now — Task 7's own test is what turns it green, and every fixture built before then would otherwise carry an empty snapshot that nothing notices. **Until Task 7 lands, comment the line out with the marker `// TASK 7: refresh_pos_cashier` and delete the comment in Task 7 Step 6.** Nothing else in the helper changes.

### Task 3: Migration 0066 — `app.branch_pos_policy`, its API, and `seedBranchPosPolicy`

**Limits are a relational row per branch, never a blob** (§4.1 of the spec, spec:1324, LEGACY-078). The till mirrors it; sync apply re-verifies against **this row**, never against the cashier's snapshot, because a snapshot is a projection and a projection can be stale by exactly the window an attacker needs.

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/db/migrations/0066_create_branch_pos_policy.sql`, `packages/db/migrations/0066.down.sql`, `packages/db/src/schema/branch-pos-policy.ts`, `packages/db/src/staff/branch-pos-policy.ts`, `packages/contracts/src/pos/branch-pos-policy.ts`, `apps/api/src/routes/pos/branch-pos-policies.ts`, `packages/db/test/staff/branch-pos-policy.test.ts`, `apps/api/test/routes/pos/branch-pos-policies.test.ts`

**Interfaces**

- Consumes: `app.company`, `app.dimension_value`, `app.catalogue_revision()`, `readCatalogue`, `currentCatalogueRevision` (6A), `withTenant` (production), `requirePermission`, `PERMISSION["admin.company.manage"]`, `IdempotencyHeader`, `ListEnvelope`, `ListQuery`, `UuidSchema`, `AppError`, `fromDatabaseError`, `RouteModule`, `buildRoutes`, `startApi`, `grantAdmin`, `IsolationProbe`.

```ts
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { startDb, withTenant, type Db } from "../harness";
import { seedTenant, seedBranch, seedBranchPosPolicy, grantCodes, type Seed } from "../fixtures";
import { lintSchema, listApplied } from "../../src";

let db: Db;
let a: Seed;
let b: Seed;

beforeAll(async () => {
  db = await startDb();
  a = await seedTenant(db, "alpha");
  b = await seedTenant(db, "beta");
  // `app.branch_pos_policy` takes Form A of `branch_isolation` in Task 5's migration 0068, and every read
  // below is `a.human`. One company-wide grant (no `branchIds`) is what keeps them non-empty afterwards —
  // amendment A15's consumer side, written now so this suite never turns red at Task 5.
  await grantCodes(db, a);
}, 180_000);
afterAll(async () => { await db.stop(); });

describe("app.branch_pos_policy — limits are a row, never a blob (§4.6.4; spec:1324)", () => {
  it("migration 66 is applied and every limit defaults to the closed position", async () => {
    expect(await listApplied(db.migrator)).toContain(66);
    const id = await seedBranchPosPolicy(db, a);
    const [row] = await withTenant(db.rw, a.tenant, a.human, (tx) => tx<Record<string, string>[]>`
      select max_line_discount_pct::text, max_ticket_discount_pct::text, max_void_amount::text,
             max_price_override_pct::text, blind_return_allowed::text, max_blind_return_amount::text,
             rounding_increment::text, paid_out_allowed::text, max_paid_out_amount::text
        from app.branch_pos_policy where id = ${id}`);
    expect(row).toEqual({
      max_line_discount_pct: "0.000000", max_ticket_discount_pct: "0.000000", max_void_amount: "0.0000",
      max_price_override_pct: "0.000000", blind_return_allowed: "false", max_blind_return_amount: "0.0000",
      rounding_increment: "0.0500", paid_out_allowed: "false", max_paid_out_amount: "0.0000",
    });
  });

  it("one row per branch — a second insert for the same branch conflicts", async () => {
    const second = await seedBranch(db, a, { code: "BR-2" });
    await seedBranchPosPolicy(db, a, { branchId: second });
    await expect(seedBranchPosPolicy(db, a, { branchId: second })).rejects.toThrow(/duplicate key/);
  });

  it("a limit is stored at its declared scale and never as a float", async () => {
    const br = await seedBranch(db, a, { code: "BR-3" });
    const id = await seedBranchPosPolicy(db, a, {
      branchId: br,
      limits: { maxLineDiscountPct: "0.150000", maxVoidAmount: "250.0000", blindReturnAllowed: true, maxBlindReturnAmount: "75.5000" },
    });
    const [row] = await withTenant(db.rw, a.tenant, a.human, (tx) => tx<Record<string, string>[]>`
      select max_line_discount_pct::text, max_void_amount::text, max_blind_return_amount::text
        from app.branch_pos_policy where id = ${id}`);
    expect(row).toEqual({ max_line_discount_pct: "0.150000", max_void_amount: "250.0000", max_blind_return_amount: "75.5000" });
  });

  it("a negative or over-100% limit is refused by a CHECK, not by a handler", async () => {
    const br = await seedBranch(db, a, { code: "BR-4" });
    await expect(seedBranchPosPolicy(db, a, { branchId: br, limits: { maxLineDiscountPct: "-0.010000" } }))
      .rejects.toThrow(/branch_pos_policy_pct_range/);
    await expect(seedBranchPosPolicy(db, a, { branchId: br, limits: { maxTicketDiscountPct: "1.500000" } }))
      .rejects.toThrow(/branch_pos_policy_pct_range/);
    await expect(seedBranchPosPolicy(db, a, { branchId: br, limits: { maxVoidAmount: "-1.0000" } }))
      .rejects.toThrow(/branch_pos_policy_amount_non_negative/);
  });

  it("writing the row bumps the catalogue revision so the branch's terminals re-pull it (§4.10.2)", async () => {
    const br = await seedBranch(db, a, { code: "BR-5" });
    const id = await seedBranchPosPolicy(db, a, { branchId: br });
    const [before] = await withTenant(db.rw, a.tenant, a.human, (tx) =>
      tx<{ r: string }[]>`select revision::text as r from app.branch_pos_policy where id = ${id}`);
    await withTenant(db.rw, a.tenant, a.human, (tx) =>
      tx`update app.branch_pos_policy set max_void_amount = 10.0000 where id = ${id}`);
    const [after] = await withTenant(db.rw, a.tenant, a.human, (tx) =>
      tx<{ r: string }[]>`select revision::text as r from app.branch_pos_policy where id = ${id}`);
    expect(BigInt(after!.r)).toBeGreaterThan(BigInt(before!.r));
  });

  it("tenant B cannot see tenant A's policy rows", async () => {
    const id = await seedBranchPosPolicy(db, a, { branchId: await seedBranch(db, a, { code: "BR-6" }) });
    const rows = await withTenant(db.rw, b.tenant, b.human, (tx) => tx`select id from app.branch_pos_policy where id = ${id}`);
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it("the schema linter reports zero violations after 0066", async () => {
    expect(await lintSchema(db.admin)).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write `0066_create_branch_pos_policy.sql`**

```sql
-- 0066_create_branch_pos_policy.sql — the per-branch POS limit row (§4.6.4; spec:1324).
-- "Limits live in a relational branch_pos_policy row per branch (§4.1: never a blob), maintained under
-- admin.company.manage; sync apply re-verifies against that row, never against the snapshot."

create table app.branch_pos_policy (
  id                       uuid primary key default uuidv7(),
  tenant_id                uuid not null references app.tenant(id),
  company_id               uuid not null,
  branch_id                uuid not null,
  max_line_discount_pct    numeric(9,6) not null default 0,
  max_ticket_discount_pct  numeric(9,6) not null default 0,
  max_void_amount          numeric(19,4) not null default 0,
  max_price_override_pct   numeric(9,6) not null default 0,
  blind_return_allowed     boolean not null default false,
  max_blind_return_amount  numeric(19,4) not null default 0,
  max_cash_in_drawer       numeric(19,4),
  require_customer_over    numeric(19,4),
  rounding_increment       numeric(19,4) not null default 0.05,
  paid_out_allowed         boolean not null default false,          -- ⚠️ OD-10: present and OFF; 6D's till-cash tasks read it
  max_paid_out_amount      numeric(19,4) not null default 0,        -- ⚠️ OD-10
  revision                 bigint not null default 0,               -- catalogue revision (§4.10.2)
  created_at               timestamptz not null default now(),
  -- Every default is the CLOSED position: a branch with no configuration authorises nothing, so a missing
  -- row and a zeroed row refuse identically at sync apply (LEGACY-027 — an invariant is never "off by default").
  constraint branch_pos_policy_pct_range check (
    max_line_discount_pct between 0 and 1 and max_ticket_discount_pct between 0 and 1
    and max_price_override_pct between 0 and 1),
  constraint branch_pos_policy_amount_non_negative check (
    max_void_amount >= 0 and max_blind_return_amount >= 0 and max_paid_out_amount >= 0
    and rounding_increment > 0
    and (max_cash_in_drawer is null or max_cash_in_drawer >= 0)
    and (require_customer_over is null or require_customer_over >= 0)),
  unique (company_id, branch_id),
  unique (tenant_id, id),
  foreign key (tenant_id, company_id) references app.company(tenant_id, id),
  foreign key (tenant_id, branch_id)  references app.dimension_value(tenant_id, id)
);
call app_meta.enable_tenant_rls('app.branch_pos_policy');
call app_meta.attach_audit('app.branch_pos_policy');   -- a limit change is exactly the write an auditor asks about

-- down-synced to the terminals of this branch only (stream 'branch_pos_policy'; spec:1312, §4.10.2)
create trigger catalogue_revision before insert or update on app.branch_pos_policy
  for each row execute function app.catalogue_revision('branch_pos_policy');
create index branch_pos_policy_cursor_idx on app.branch_pos_policy (company_id, branch_id, revision);

-- ── the down-sync stream 6A's readCatalogue calls (§4.10.2; 6A:2913) ─────────────────────────────────
-- The twin of 0065's app.pos_cashier_sync, and it ships in the SAME file as its table for the same reason:
-- readCatalogue probes `to_regclass('app.branch_pos_policy')` and executes
--   select ... from app.branch_pos_policy_sync($company, $branch, $since)
-- as soon as that is not null. 6A fixes the signature; this function is the only thing 6C changes when the
-- table's columns settle. There is no `archived_at` and no revocation here: a limit row is edited, never
-- deleted (spec:2049), so this stream has no tombstone and no exclusion.
create function app.branch_pos_policy_sync(p_company_id uuid, p_branch_id uuid, p_since bigint)
returns table (id uuid, revision bigint, row jsonb)
language sql stable as $$
  select b.id, b.revision, to_jsonb(b) - 'tenant_id'
    from app.branch_pos_policy b
   where b.company_id = p_company_id
     and b.branch_id = p_branch_id
     and b.revision > p_since
$$;
grant execute on function app.branch_pos_policy_sync(uuid, uuid, bigint) to app_rw;
```

`packages/db/migrations/0066.down.sql`:

```sql
drop function if exists app.branch_pos_policy_sync(uuid, uuid, bigint);
drop index if exists app.branch_pos_policy_cursor_idx;
drop trigger if exists catalogue_revision on app.branch_pos_policy;
drop table if exists app.branch_pos_policy;
```

**Percentages are fractions, not percents.** `0.150000` is fifteen per cent, and the `between 0 and 1` CHECK is what makes that unambiguous for every reader and every till. A limit stored as `15` would authorise a fifteen-hundred-per-cent discount the first time somebody divided instead of multiplying — the arithmetic half of LEGACY-020 in a different module.

**6A wrote scaffolding this task is required to remove.** `packages/db/src/sync/catalogue.ts` reads the two 6C streams through a **second statement** guarded by a `to_regclass` capability probe, and 6A says so twice — in the function's own doc comment (*"6C's first task moves them into this `union all`"*) and in its ⚠️ paragraph (*"**6C's first task deletes the whole `extra` block**"*). Both functions now exist (0065 section 4, 0066 above), so the guard, the second query and the merge-sort all go.

Delete the `const [caps] = …` probe, the whole `// ── 6C's two streams, as a second query ──` block including `extra` and `merged`, and add the two legs to the `live` CTE where 6A's comment marks their place:

```ts
      union all
      -- 6C's two streams, folded in by 6C's Task 3 — the `extra` block and its to_regclass probe are gone.
      -- The FUNCTIONS are what this query names, not the tables: 6C owns their column lists and changes one
      -- function body when its schema settles, without touching this file (6A's reason, kept).
      select 'cashier', k.id, k.revision, k.row
        from app.pos_cashier_sync(${input.companyId}, ${input.branchId}, ${input.since}) k
      union all
      select 'branch_pos_policy', b.id, b.revision, b.row
        from app.branch_pos_policy_sync(${input.companyId}, ${input.branchId}, ${input.since}) b
```

and replace the three lines that consumed `merged` with the ones that consume `rows` directly:

```ts
  const hasMore = rows.length > input.limit;
  const page = hasMore ? rows.slice(0, input.limit) : rows;
```

**`currentCatalogueRevision` gains the same two legs, and forgetting them is a silent staleness bug.** 6A's version unions the maxima of `item`, `price_list`, `promotion`, `party`, `tax_code` and `catalogue_tombstone` only (6A's Task 8). `BatchOutcome.catalogueRevision` is what tells a till how stale it is, so a company whose only change since the last pull was a PIN reset or a limit edit would be told it is current and would never pull the change:

```ts
      union all select max(revision) from app.pos_cashier where company_id = ${companyId}
      union all select max(revision) from app.branch_pos_policy where company_id = ${companyId}
```

Then re-run **6A's own suite**, which is what proves the fold: `bun run --cwd packages/db test -- sync/catalogue`. 6A's case that exercised the probe path — the one asserting a `cashier` change appears in the page — now runs through the `union all` and must still pass unchanged; if 6A wrote that case against the two-query shape (asserting the order the merge-sort produced), **update the assertion and not the query**, because a single ordered `union all` is the shape 6A's own comment asks for.

- [ ] **Step 4: Add `seedBranchPosPolicy` to `fixtures.ts` and the Drizzle mirror**

```ts
/**
 * One `branch_pos_policy` row. Every unspecified limit keeps the migration's CLOSED default.
 *
 * `limits` carries **every one of §4.6.4's eleven settable columns**, including the two nullable ones
 * (`maxCashInDrawer`, `requireCustomerOver`) — the same key set as `BranchPosPolicyInput` minus its
 * required `branchId`, which this helper takes as its own option. It is spelled out rather than imported
 * from `../src/staff/branch-pos-policy`, because that module is written in Step 6 and this file is run
 * green in Step 4; the two shapes are asserted equal by `typecheck` at the route layer, which passes a
 * `BranchPosPolicyInput` into the same columns.
 *
 * **It inserts as `db.migrator` / `s.system` — amendment A15.** `app.branch_pos_policy` carries Form A of
 * the RESTRICTIVE `branch_isolation` family from migration 0068, `app.actor_in_branch` is true only for a
 * `kind = 'system'` principal or one with a live `app.role_assignment`, `seedTenant` grants `s.human`
 * nothing, and a `using`-only policy is applied AS the `with check` on INSERT.
 */
export async function seedBranchPosPolicy(
  db: Db, s: Seed,
  opts: {
    branchId?: string;
    limits?: {
      maxLineDiscountPct?: string; maxTicketDiscountPct?: string; maxVoidAmount?: string;
      maxPriceOverridePct?: string; blindReturnAllowed?: boolean; maxBlindReturnAmount?: string;
      maxCashInDrawer?: string | null; requireCustomerOver?: string | null; roundingIncrement?: string;
      paidOutAllowed?: boolean; maxPaidOutAmount?: string;
    };
  } = {},
): Promise<string> {
  const id = await uuidv7(db.admin);
  const l = opts.limits ?? {};
  await withTenant(db.migrator, s.tenant, s.system, (tx) => tx`
    insert into app.branch_pos_policy
      (id, tenant_id, company_id, branch_id, max_line_discount_pct, max_ticket_discount_pct, max_void_amount,
       max_price_override_pct, blind_return_allowed, max_blind_return_amount, max_cash_in_drawer,
       require_customer_over, rounding_increment, paid_out_allowed, max_paid_out_amount)
    values (${id}, ${s.tenant}, ${s.company}, ${opts.branchId ?? s.hq},
            ${l.maxLineDiscountPct ?? "0.000000"}, ${l.maxTicketDiscountPct ?? "0.000000"}, ${l.maxVoidAmount ?? "0.0000"},
            ${l.maxPriceOverridePct ?? "0.000000"}, ${l.blindReturnAllowed ?? false}, ${l.maxBlindReturnAmount ?? "0.0000"},
            ${l.maxCashInDrawer ?? null}, ${l.requireCustomerOver ?? null},
            ${l.roundingIncrement ?? "0.0500"}, ${l.paidOutAllowed ?? false}, ${l.maxPaidOutAmount ?? "0.0000"})`);
  return id;
}
```

```ts
import { bigint, boolean, numeric, pgSchema, timestamp, uuid } from "drizzle-orm/pg-core";

const app = pgSchema("app");

export const branchPosPolicy = app.table("branch_pos_policy", {
  id: uuid("id").primaryKey(),
  tenantId: uuid("tenant_id").notNull(),
  companyId: uuid("company_id").notNull(),
  branchId: uuid("branch_id").notNull(),
  maxLineDiscountPct: numeric("max_line_discount_pct", { precision: 9, scale: 6 }).notNull(),
  maxTicketDiscountPct: numeric("max_ticket_discount_pct", { precision: 9, scale: 6 }).notNull(),
  maxVoidAmount: numeric("max_void_amount", { precision: 19, scale: 4 }).notNull(),
  maxPriceOverridePct: numeric("max_price_override_pct", { precision: 9, scale: 6 }).notNull(),
  blindReturnAllowed: boolean("blind_return_allowed").notNull(),
  maxBlindReturnAmount: numeric("max_blind_return_amount", { precision: 19, scale: 4 }).notNull(),
  maxCashInDrawer: numeric("max_cash_in_drawer", { precision: 19, scale: 4 }),
  requireCustomerOver: numeric("require_customer_over", { precision: 19, scale: 4 }),
  roundingIncrement: numeric("rounding_increment", { precision: 19, scale: 4 }).notNull(),
  paidOutAllowed: boolean("paid_out_allowed").notNull(),
  maxPaidOutAmount: numeric("max_paid_out_amount", { precision: 19, scale: 4 }).notNull(),
  revision: bigint("revision", { mode: "bigint" }).notNull(),
  createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull(),
});
```

`packages/contracts/src/pos/branch-pos-policy.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from "@hono/zod-openapi";
import { ListEnvelope, ListQuery, UuidSchema } from "../common";

/** A fraction, not a percent: "0.150000" is fifteen per cent (0066's `between 0 and 1` CHECK). */
const Pct = z.string().regex(/^0\.\d{6}$|^1\.000000$/, "a 6-dp fraction between 0.000000 and 1.000000");
/** A non-negative 4-dp amount in the company's base currency. `no-number-money` forbids the `number` form. */
const Amount = z.string().regex(/^\d+\.\d{4}$/);

export const BranchPosPolicySchema = z.object({
  id: UuidSchema,
  branchId: UuidSchema,
  maxLineDiscountPct: Pct,
  maxTicketDiscountPct: Pct,
  maxVoidAmount: Amount,
  maxPriceOverridePct: Pct,
  blindReturnAllowed: z.boolean(),
  maxBlindReturnAmount: Amount,
  maxCashInDrawer: Amount.nullable(),
  requireCustomerOver: Amount.nullable(),
  roundingIncrement: Amount,
  paidOutAllowed: z.boolean(),
  maxPaidOutAmount: Amount,
  revision: z.string(),          // a bigint on the wire is a decimal STRING, never a JSON number
}).openapi("BranchPosPolicy");

export const UpsertBranchPosPolicyBody = BranchPosPolicySchema
  .omit({ id: true, revision: true })
  .partial()
  .extend({ branchId: UuidSchema })
  .openapi("UpsertBranchPosPolicyBody");

export const BranchPosPolicyQuery = ListQuery.extend({
  "filter[branchId][eq]": UuidSchema.optional(),      // §4.2.8 Layer 2: a literal filter key, typed and in the OpenAPI document
});
export const BranchPosPolicyList = ListEnvelope(BranchPosPolicySchema);
export type BranchPosPolicyDto = z.infer<typeof BranchPosPolicySchema>;
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write the repository and the route module**

`packages/db/src/staff/branch-pos-policy.ts` — every statement a `tx` tagged template, every numeric read as `::text` so no `number` ever holds a limit:

```ts
import type { Sql } from "postgres";
import type { Cursor } from "@dulja/contracts";

export interface BranchPosPolicyRow {
  id: string; companyId: string; branchId: string;
  maxLineDiscountPct: string; maxTicketDiscountPct: string; maxVoidAmount: string;
  maxPriceOverridePct: string; blindReturnAllowed: boolean; maxBlindReturnAmount: string;
  maxCashInDrawer: string | null; requireCustomerOver: string | null; roundingIncrement: string;
  paidOutAllowed: boolean; maxPaidOutAmount: string;
  revision: string; createdAt: string;
}

export interface BranchPosPolicyInput {
  branchId: string;
  maxLineDiscountPct?: string; maxTicketDiscountPct?: string; maxVoidAmount?: string;
  maxPriceOverridePct?: string; blindReturnAllowed?: boolean; maxBlindReturnAmount?: string;
  maxCashInDrawer?: string | null; requireCustomerOver?: string | null; roundingIncrement?: string;
  paidOutAllowed?: boolean; maxPaidOutAmount?: string;
}

const SELECT = (tx: Sql) => tx`
  select id, company_id as "companyId", branch_id as "branchId",
         max_line_discount_pct::text   as "maxLineDiscountPct",
         max_ticket_discount_pct::text as "maxTicketDiscountPct",
         max_void_amount::text         as "maxVoidAmount",
         max_price_override_pct::text  as "maxPriceOverridePct",
         blind_return_allowed          as "blindReturnAllowed",
         max_blind_return_amount::text as "maxBlindReturnAmount",
         max_cash_in_drawer::text      as "maxCashInDrawer",
         require_customer_over::text   as "requireCustomerOver",
         rounding_increment::text      as "roundingIncrement",
         paid_out_allowed              as "paidOutAllowed",
         max_paid_out_amount::text     as "maxPaidOutAmount",
         revision::text                as "revision",
         created_at                    as "createdAt"
    from app.branch_pos_policy`;

/** One policy per branch. A second PUT for the same branch edits the row and bumps its revision. */
export async function upsertBranchPosPolicy(tx: Sql, companyId: string, input: BranchPosPolicyInput): Promise<BranchPosPolicyRow> {
  const [row] = await tx<BranchPosPolicyRow[]>`
    insert into app.branch_pos_policy
      (tenant_id, company_id, branch_id, max_line_discount_pct, max_ticket_discount_pct, max_void_amount,
       max_price_override_pct, blind_return_allowed, max_blind_return_amount, max_cash_in_drawer,
       require_customer_over, rounding_increment, paid_out_allowed, max_paid_out_amount)
    values (app.current_tenant(), ${companyId}, ${input.branchId},
            coalesce(${input.maxLineDiscountPct ?? null}::numeric, 0),
            coalesce(${input.maxTicketDiscountPct ?? null}::numeric, 0),
            coalesce(${input.maxVoidAmount ?? null}::numeric, 0),
            coalesce(${input.maxPriceOverridePct ?? null}::numeric, 0),
            coalesce(${input.blindReturnAllowed ?? null}::boolean, false),
            coalesce(${input.maxBlindReturnAmount ?? null}::numeric, 0),
            ${input.maxCashInDrawer ?? null}::numeric,
            ${input.requireCustomerOver ?? null}::numeric,
            coalesce(${input.roundingIncrement ?? null}::numeric, 0.05),
            coalesce(${input.paidOutAllowed ?? null}::boolean, false),
            coalesce(${input.maxPaidOutAmount ?? null}::numeric, 0))
    on conflict (company_id, branch_id) do update set
      max_line_discount_pct   = coalesce(${input.maxLineDiscountPct ?? null}::numeric, app.branch_pos_policy.max_line_discount_pct),
      max_ticket_discount_pct = coalesce(${input.maxTicketDiscountPct ?? null}::numeric, app.branch_pos_policy.max_ticket_discount_pct),
      max_void_amount         = coalesce(${input.maxVoidAmount ?? null}::numeric, app.branch_pos_policy.max_void_amount),
      max_price_override_pct  = coalesce(${input.maxPriceOverridePct ?? null}::numeric, app.branch_pos_policy.max_price_override_pct),
      blind_return_allowed    = coalesce(${input.blindReturnAllowed ?? null}::boolean, app.branch_pos_policy.blind_return_allowed),
      max_blind_return_amount = coalesce(${input.maxBlindReturnAmount ?? null}::numeric, app.branch_pos_policy.max_blind_return_amount),
      max_cash_in_drawer      = ${input.maxCashInDrawer ?? null}::numeric,
      require_customer_over   = ${input.requireCustomerOver ?? null}::numeric,
      rounding_increment      = coalesce(${input.roundingIncrement ?? null}::numeric, app.branch_pos_policy.rounding_increment),
      paid_out_allowed        = coalesce(${input.paidOutAllowed ?? null}::boolean, app.branch_pos_policy.paid_out_allowed),
      max_paid_out_amount     = coalesce(${input.maxPaidOutAmount ?? null}::numeric, app.branch_pos_policy.max_paid_out_amount)
    returning id, company_id as "companyId", branch_id as "branchId",
              max_line_discount_pct::text as "maxLineDiscountPct", max_ticket_discount_pct::text as "maxTicketDiscountPct",
              max_void_amount::text as "maxVoidAmount", max_price_override_pct::text as "maxPriceOverridePct",
              blind_return_allowed as "blindReturnAllowed", max_blind_return_amount::text as "maxBlindReturnAmount",
              max_cash_in_drawer::text as "maxCashInDrawer", require_customer_over::text as "requireCustomerOver",
              rounding_increment::text as "roundingIncrement", paid_out_allowed as "paidOutAllowed",
              max_paid_out_amount::text as "maxPaidOutAmount", revision::text as "revision", created_at as "createdAt"`;
  return row!;
}

/**
 * `SELECT(tx)` returns a postgres.js **query object**, not a function, so it can only be INTERPOLATED into
 * another tagged template — `` tx`${SELECT(tx)} where …` `` — never called as a tag itself. The earlier
 * form, `` SELECT(tx)`where …` ``, is a `TypeError: SELECT(...) is not a function` at the first call, and
 * an `as unknown as BranchPosPolicyRow[]` cast is what would hide it from `typecheck`. There is no cast
 * here for exactly that reason: the tag's own type parameter carries the shape.
 */
export async function getBranchPosPolicy(tx: Sql, companyId: string, branchId: string): Promise<BranchPosPolicyRow | null> {
  const rows = await tx<BranchPosPolicyRow[]>`
    ${SELECT(tx)} where company_id = ${companyId} and branch_id = ${branchId}`;
  return rows[0] ?? null;
}

export async function listBranchPosPolicies(
  tx: Sql, companyId: string, opts: { limit: number; cursor?: Cursor; branchId?: string },
): Promise<{ rows: BranchPosPolicyRow[]; nextCursor: string | null }> {
  const rows = await tx<BranchPosPolicyRow[]>`
    ${SELECT(tx)}
     where company_id = ${companyId}
       and (${opts.branchId ?? null}::uuid is null or branch_id = ${opts.branchId ?? null}::uuid)
       and (${opts.cursor?.id ?? null}::uuid is null or id > ${opts.cursor?.id ?? null}::uuid)
     order by id
     limit ${opts.limit + 1}`;
  const page = rows.slice(0, opts.limit);
  return { rows: page, nextCursor: rows.length > opts.limit ? page[page.length - 1]!.id : null };
}
```

`apps/api/src/routes/pos/branch-pos-policies.ts`:

```ts
import { createRoute, OpenAPIHono, z } from "@hono/zod-openapi";
import { PERMISSION } from "@dulja/contracts/permissions";
import {
  BranchPosPolicyList, BranchPosPolicyQuery, BranchPosPolicySchema, UpsertBranchPosPolicyBody,
} from "@dulja/contracts/pos/branch-pos-policy";
import { ProblemSchema, encodeCursor, decodeCursor } from "@dulja/contracts";
import { getBranchPosPolicy, listBranchPosPolicies, upsertBranchPosPolicy } from "@dulja/db";
import { AppError } from "@dulja/contracts";
import type { AppEnv } from "../../context";
import type { RouteModule } from "../index";
import { IdempotencyHeader } from "../index";
import { requirePermission } from "../../middleware/require-permission";

export const branchPosPolicyRoutes = new OpenAPIHono<AppEnv>();
const scope = { company: "path:companyId", branch: "body:branchId" } as const;
const listScope = { company: "path:companyId" } as const;
const Params = z.object({ companyId: z.string().uuid() });

branchPosPolicyRoutes.openapi(
  createRoute({
    method: "get", path: "/", tags: ["pos"],
    request: { params: Params, query: BranchPosPolicyQuery },
    middleware: [requirePermission(PERMISSION["admin.company.manage"], listScope)],
    responses: {
      200: { description: "branch POS policies", content: { "application/json": { schema: BranchPosPolicyList } } },
      403: { description: "forbidden", content: { "application/problem+json": { schema: ProblemSchema } } },
    },
  }),
  async (c) => {
    const { companyId } = c.req.valid("param");
    const q = c.req.valid("query");
    const cursor = q.cursor ? decodeCursor(q.cursor) : undefined;
    const { rows, nextCursor } = await c.var.withTenant((tx) =>
      listBranchPosPolicies(tx, companyId, { limit: q.limit, cursor, branchId: q["filter[branchId][eq]"] }));
    return c.json({ items: rows, next_cursor: nextCursor ? encodeCursor({ k: [], id: nextCursor }) : null }, 200);
  },
);

branchPosPolicyRoutes.openapi(
  createRoute({
    method: "put", path: "/", tags: ["pos"],
    request: {
      params: Params, headers: IdempotencyHeader,
      body: { content: { "application/json": { schema: UpsertBranchPosPolicyBody } } },
    },
    middleware: [requirePermission(PERMISSION["admin.company.manage"], scope)],
    responses: {
      200: { description: "the policy for this branch", content: { "application/json": { schema: BranchPosPolicySchema } } },
      403: { description: "forbidden", content: { "application/problem+json": { schema: ProblemSchema } } },
      404: { description: "no such branch", content: { "application/problem+json": { schema: ProblemSchema } } },
    },
  }),
  async (c) => {
    const { companyId } = c.req.valid("param");
    const body = c.req.valid("json");
    const row = await c.var.withTenant(async (tx) => {
      // RLS has already reduced dimension_value to this tenant; a branch the caller cannot see is a 404,
      // never a 403 that would confirm the row exists in another branch (§4.2.4's ERR_NOT_FOUND rule).
      const [branch] = await tx`select 1 from app.dimension_value where id = ${body.branchId}`;
      if (!branch) throw new AppError("ERR_NOT_FOUND", `branch ${body.branchId} is not visible`);
      return upsertBranchPosPolicy(tx, companyId, body);
    });
    return c.json(row, 200);
  },
);

export const branchPosPolicyRouteModule: RouteModule = {
  path: "/v1/companies/:companyId/branch-pos-policies",
  router: branchPosPolicyRoutes,
  permissions: [PERMISSION["admin.company.manage"]],
  auth: "human",
};
```

Register it inside `buildRoutes` (never by pushing onto `ROUTES` — §4.2.7) and add the probe:

```ts
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { startApi, type StartedApi } from "../../support/app";
import { seedTenant, seedBranch, type Seed } from "@dulja/db/test";
import { grantAdmin, problemOf } from "../../../../../tests/support/api";

let api: StartedApi;
let s: Seed;
let branch: string;

beforeAll(async () => {
  api = await startApi();
  s = await seedTenant(api.db, "pol");
  branch = await seedBranch(api.db, s, { code: "BR-API" });
  await grantAdmin(api, s);
  await api.loadPrincipals();
}, 180_000);
afterAll(async () => { await api.stop(); });

describe("PUT|GET /v1/companies/{companyId}/branch-pos-policies", () => {
  it("creates the row, then edits the same row on a second PUT", async () => {
    const first = await api.request({ method: "PUT", path: `/v1/companies/${s.company}/branch-pos-policies`, as: s.human,
      body: { branchId: branch, maxLineDiscountPct: "0.100000", maxVoidAmount: "50.0000" } });
    expect(first.status).toBe(200);
    const a = await first.json() as { id: string; maxLineDiscountPct: string; revision: string };
    expect(a.maxLineDiscountPct).toBe("0.100000");

    const second = await api.request({ method: "PUT", path: `/v1/companies/${s.company}/branch-pos-policies`, as: s.human,
      body: { branchId: branch, maxVoidAmount: "75.0000" } });
    const b = await second.json() as { id: string; maxLineDiscountPct: string; maxVoidAmount: string; revision: string };
    expect(b.id).toBe(a.id);                          // one row per branch
    expect(b.maxLineDiscountPct).toBe("0.100000");    // an omitted field keeps its value
    expect(b.maxVoidAmount).toBe("75.0000");
    expect(BigInt(b.revision)).toBeGreaterThan(BigInt(a.revision));
  });

  it("refuses an unsafe call with no Idempotency-Key (spec:2055)", async () => {
    const res = await api.request({ method: "PUT", path: `/v1/companies/${s.company}/branch-pos-policies`, as: s.human,
      body: { branchId: branch }, idempotencyKey: null });
    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
    expect((await problemOf(res)).code).toBe("ERR_IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_MISSING");
  });

  it("a percentage above 1.000000 fails Zod before it reaches the database", async () => {
    const res = await api.request({ method: "PUT", path: `/v1/companies/${s.company}/branch-pos-policies`, as: s.human,
      body: { branchId: branch, maxTicketDiscountPct: "1.500000" } });
    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
    const p = await problemOf(res);
    expect(p.code).toBe("ERR_VALIDATION_FAILED");
    expect(p.errors?.[0]?.path).toContain("maxTicketDiscountPct");
  });

  it("a branch of another tenant is 404, never 403 (no cross-tenant existence oracle)", async () => {
    const other = await seedTenant(api.db, "pol-other");
    const foreign = await seedBranch(api.db, other, { code: "BR-X" });
    const res = await api.request({ method: "PUT", path: `/v1/companies/${s.company}/branch-pos-policies`, as: s.human,
      body: { branchId: foreign } });
    expect(res.status).toBe(404);
    expect((await problemOf(res)).code).toBe("ERR_NOT_FOUND");
  });

  it("a principal without admin.company.manage is 403", async () => {
    const plain = await seedTenant(api.db, "pol-plain");
    await api.loadPrincipals();
    const res = await api.request({ method: "GET", path: `/v1/companies/${plain.company}/branch-pos-policies`, as: plain.human });
    expect(res.status).toBe(403);
    expect((await problemOf(res)).code).toBe("ERR_RBAC_FORBIDDEN");
  });
});
```

### Task 4: Migration 0067 — `app.attendance_event` and the back-office correction route

**An attendance event is attributed to the employee and acted by the terminal or the principal** (spec:2089, hazard 19). It is **never an automatic pay input in v1** — 8B reads it as a report and computes nothing from it, and this task's DDL comment says so where a future reader will find it.

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/db/migrations/0067_create_attendance_event_and_cashier_snapshot.sql` (sections 1–2; Task 7 appends 3–5 in the same PR — **the long name is §3.4.2's round-2 rule and the file carries it from its first commit**), `packages/db/migrations/0067.down.sql`, `packages/db/src/schema/attendance-event.ts`, `packages/db/src/staff/attendance.ts`, `packages/contracts/src/hr/attendance.ts`, `apps/api/src/routes/hr/attendance-events.ts`, `packages/db/test/staff/attendance-event.test.ts`, `apps/api/test/routes/hr/attendance-events.test.ts`
- Modify: `apps/api/src/routes/index.ts`, `tests/invariants/probes.ts`, **`packages/db/src/sync/apply.ts`** (Step 6 — the attendance seam replaces 6A's refusal), **`packages/db/test/sync/apply.test.ts`** (6A's own case, rewritten in the same commit), `packages/db/test/fixtures.ts` (`seedCashier` gains `principalId?` — A12), `tests/invariants/support/pos.ts` (one `seedCashier` line so 6A's cashier has a `pos_cashier` row)

**Interfaces**

- Consumes: `app.employee` (T1), `app.pos_cashier` (T2), `app.pos_terminal`, `app.dimension_value`, `app_meta.attach_stamp`, `applyBatch`, `TerminalApplyContext`, `SyncError` (6A — Step 6's seam), `posFixture`, `buildTicket`, `seedCashier` (T2), `seedEmployee` (T1), `startDb`, `withTenant` (test, in `packages/db/test`; the production 3-argument form in the route test), `uuidv7`, `seedTenant`, `seedTenantV2` (1A — the route test needs the JOURNAL policy `startApi`'s company carries), `seedBranch` (0D), `grantCodes` (2B — both the route test's grants and Step 1's `beforeAll`, so the suite survives T5's Form A policy), `startApi`, `grantAdmin`, `problemOf` (0B, §4.17.1 — the route test's `Problem` reader), `IsolationProbe`, `lintSchema`, `listApplied`, `PERMISSION["hr.attendance.record"]`, `PERMISSION["hr.employee.read"]` (both seeded in T6 — this task's routes land in the same PR sequence and T6 runs before the route test; see the Execution handoff's order).

  ```ts
  // packages/db/src/staff/attendance.ts
  export type AttendanceKind = "clock_in" | "clock_out" | "manual_correction";
  export interface AttendanceEventRow {
    id: string; employeeId: string; branchId: string; kind: AttendanceKind;
    occurredAt: string; terminalId: string | null; recordedBy: string;
    note: string | null; correctsEventId: string | null; createdAt: string;
  }
  export interface RecordAttendanceInput {
    employeeId: string; kind: AttendanceKind; occurredAt: string;
    branchId?: string; terminalId?: string | null; note?: string | null; correctsEventId?: string | null;
  }
  /** The ONE insert path. `recorded_by` is stamped by the database, never taken from `input` (LEGACY-044).
   *  Returns `null` when the `insert … select` matched no employee — not visible to this actor under RLS,
   *  or in another company — which both callers turn into their own refusal. The return is nullable and
   *  not a non-null assertion because the zero-row case is the ordinary one, not an impossibility. */
  export async function recordAttendance(tx: Sql, companyId: string, input: RecordAttendanceInput): Promise<AttendanceEventRow | null>;
  export async function listAttendance(tx: Sql, companyId: string, opts: { limit: number; cursor?: Cursor; employeeId?: string; from?: string; to?: string }): Promise<{ rows: AttendanceEventRow[]; nextCursor: string | null }>;
  ```

```ts
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { startDb, uuidv7, withTenant, type Db } from "../harness";
import { seedTenant, seedBranch, seedEmployee, grantCodes, type Seed } from "../fixtures";
import { lintSchema, listApplied } from "../../src";

let db: Db;
let a: Seed;
let b: Seed;
let employee: string;

beforeAll(async () => {
  db = await startDb();
  a = await seedTenant(db, "alpha");
  b = await seedTenant(db, "beta");
  // `app.attendance_event` takes Form A of `branch_isolation` in Task 5's migration 0068 and every insert
  // below is written as `a.human` DELIBERATELY — the whole point of the first case is that `recorded_by`
  // is stamped with the ACTING principal, and the acting principal has to be a human for the assertion to
  // mean anything. One company-wide grant (no `branchIds`) is what lets that human still pass
  // `app.actor_in_branch` after 0068, and it changes nothing about what is asserted. Amendment A15 names
  // this as the remedy for a suite whose actor must stay `s.human`; switching the actor to `s.system`
  // instead would quietly turn the stamp case into an assertion about the system principal.
  await grantCodes(db, a);
  employee = await seedEmployee(db, a, { employeeNumber: "E-ATT" });
}, 180_000);
afterAll(async () => { await db.stop(); });

const insert = (s: Seed, actor: string, cols: Record<string, unknown>) =>
  withTenant(db.rw, s.tenant, actor, (tx) => tx`
    insert into app.attendance_event (tenant_id, company_id, employee_id, branch_id, kind, occurred_at, terminal_id, recorded_by, note, corrects_event_id)
    values (${s.tenant}, ${s.company}, ${cols.employee_id as string}, ${cols.branch_id as string}, ${cols.kind as string},
            ${cols.occurred_at as string}, ${(cols.terminal_id as string) ?? null},
            ${(cols.recorded_by as string) ?? actor}, ${(cols.note as string) ?? null}, ${(cols.corrects_event_id as string) ?? null})
    returning id, recorded_by`);

describe("app.attendance_event (§4.6.1; spec:2089)", () => {
  it("migration 67 is applied and the kind vocabulary is a CHECK, not an enum (§3.4.4)", async () => {
    expect(await listApplied(db.migrator)).toContain(67);
    const [chk] = await db.admin<{ def: string }[]>`
      select pg_get_constraintdef(oid) as def from pg_constraint
       where conrelid = 'app.attendance_event'::regclass and conname = 'attendance_event_kind_check'`;
    expect(chk!.def).toContain("clock_in");
    expect(chk!.def).toContain("clock_out");
    expect(chk!.def).toContain("manual_correction");
    const [enumCount] = await db.admin<{ n: number }[]>`
      select count(*)::int as n from pg_type where typnamespace = 'app'::regnamespace and typname = 'attendance_kind'`;
    expect(enumCount!.n).toBe(0);
  });

  it("recorded_by is STAMPED — a supplied value is overwritten with the acting principal (LEGACY-044)", async () => {
    const [row] = await insert(a, a.human, {
      employee_id: employee, branch_id: a.hq, kind: "clock_in",
      occurred_at: "2026-01-15T01:00:00Z", recorded_by: a.system,     // a lie the database refuses to believe
    });
    expect(row!.recorded_by).toBe(a.human);
  });

  it("an unknown kind is refused by the CHECK", async () => {
    await expect(insert(a, a.human, { employee_id: employee, branch_id: a.hq, kind: "lunch", occurred_at: "2026-01-15T05:00:00Z" }))
      .rejects.toThrow(/attendance_event_kind_check/);
  });

  it("a correction points at an event of the same employee", async () => {
    const [orig] = await insert(a, a.human, { employee_id: employee, branch_id: a.hq, kind: "clock_out", occurred_at: "2026-01-15T09:00:00Z" });
    const other = await seedEmployee(db, a, { employeeNumber: "E-ATT2" });
    await expect(insert(a, a.human, {
      employee_id: other, branch_id: a.hq, kind: "manual_correction",
      occurred_at: "2026-01-15T09:05:00Z", corrects_event_id: orig!.id,
    })).rejects.toThrow(/attendance_event_corrects_same_employee/);
    await expect(insert(a, a.human, {
      employee_id: employee, branch_id: a.hq, kind: "manual_correction",
      occurred_at: "2026-01-15T09:05:00Z", corrects_event_id: orig!.id,
    })).resolves.toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("a correction must BE a manual_correction — a clock_in cannot correct anything", async () => {
    const [orig] = await insert(a, a.human, { employee_id: employee, branch_id: a.hq, kind: "clock_in", occurred_at: "2026-01-16T01:00:00Z" });
    await expect(insert(a, a.human, {
      employee_id: employee, branch_id: a.hq, kind: "clock_in",
      occurred_at: "2026-01-16T01:05:00Z", corrects_event_id: orig!.id,
    })).rejects.toThrow(/attendance_event_correction_kind/);

    const rows = await withTenant(db.rw, b.tenant, b.human, (tx) =>
      tx`select id from app.attendance_event where employee_id = ${employee}`);
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it("the schema linter reports zero violations after 0067", async () => {
    expect(await lintSchema(db.admin)).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write `0067_create_attendance_event_and_cashier_snapshot.sql`, sections 1–2**

**The file is created under its final, longer name here**, because **Task 7 appends sections 3–5** (the snapshot function, its four triggers and the `who_can_reset_a_pin` view) to this same file — 6C's range is 0064–0069 and every number is used, so there is no seventh migration to put them in, and §3.4.2's round-2 rule makes the longer name the right one from the first commit rather than a rename later. Tasks 4 and 7 are two commits in **one** PR named for this file.

```sql
-- 0067_create_attendance_event_and_cashier_snapshot.sql — minimal attendance (§4.6.1; spec:2089)
-- in sections 1-2, and the pos_cashier snapshot projection (§4.6.3; spec:1324) in sections 3-5 (Task 7).
-- "Attributed to the employee, with the terminal or principal as actor." Readable by HR and payroll as a
-- report; NEVER an automatic pay input in v1 — 8B computes nothing from these rows, and unpaid leave
-- reaches payroll as a quantity on a pay component instead (spec:2089, 2079).

create table app.attendance_event (
  id                uuid primary key default uuidv7(),
  tenant_id         uuid not null references app.tenant(id),
  company_id        uuid not null,
  employee_id       uuid not null,                                   -- ATTRIBUTION: whose attendance this is
  branch_id         uuid not null,                                   -- where; branch_isolation is attached by 0068 (Form A)
  kind              text not null,
  occurred_at       timestamptz not null,                            -- business time: the till clock or the corrected time
  terminal_id       uuid,                                            -- the till that recorded it, when it came from one
  recorded_by       uuid not null default app.current_actor() references app.principal(id),  -- ACTOR: stamped, never a payload value (LEGACY-044)
  note              text,
  corrects_event_id uuid,
  created_at        timestamptz not null default now(),
  constraint attendance_event_kind_check check (kind in ('clock_in', 'clock_out', 'manual_correction')),
  -- Only a manual_correction corrects anything, and only a manual_correction may carry the pointer.
  constraint attendance_event_correction_kind check (
    (corrects_event_id is null) or (kind = 'manual_correction')),
  unique (tenant_id, id),
  foreign key (tenant_id, company_id)        references app.company(tenant_id, id),
  foreign key (tenant_id, employee_id)       references app.employee(tenant_id, id),
  foreign key (tenant_id, branch_id)         references app.dimension_value(tenant_id, id),
  foreign key (tenant_id, terminal_id)       references app.pos_terminal(tenant_id, id),
  foreign key (tenant_id, corrects_event_id) references app.attendance_event(tenant_id, id)
);
call app_meta.enable_tenant_rls('app.attendance_event');
call app_meta.attach_audit('app.attendance_event');
call app_meta.attach_stamp('app.attendance_event', 'recorded_by');

-- A correction that points at another employee's event would silently move hours between two people.
-- A composite FK cannot express it (the parent's employee_id is not part of any key), so it is a trigger.
create function app.assert_attendance_correction() returns trigger language plpgsql as $$
declare target_employee uuid;
begin
  if new.corrects_event_id is null then return new; end if;
  select employee_id into target_employee from app.attendance_event where id = new.corrects_event_id;
  if target_employee is null then
    raise exception 'ERR_NOT_FOUND: attendance event % is not visible', new.corrects_event_id using errcode = 'check_violation';
  end if;
  if target_employee <> new.employee_id then
    raise exception 'attendance_event_corrects_same_employee: event % belongs to employee %, not %',
      new.corrects_event_id, target_employee, new.employee_id using errcode = 'check_violation';
  end if;
  return new;
end $$;

create trigger attendance_event_correction before insert on app.attendance_event
  for each row execute function app.assert_attendance_correction();

create index attendance_event_employee_idx on app.attendance_event (employee_id, occurred_at desc);
create index attendance_event_branch_idx on app.attendance_event (company_id, branch_id, occurred_at desc);

-- Sections 3-5 (the pos_cashier snapshot projection, its four triggers and app.who_can_reset_a_pin) are
-- appended by Task 7, in this same file and this same PR.
```

`packages/db/migrations/0067.down.sql` — sections 3–5's drops are prepended to it by Task 7:

```sql
drop trigger if exists attendance_event_correction on app.attendance_event;
drop function if exists app.assert_attendance_correction() cascade;
drop table if exists app.attendance_event;
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the repository, the mirror and the contracts module**

`packages/db/src/staff/attendance.ts`:

```ts
import type { Sql } from "postgres";
import type { Cursor } from "@dulja/contracts";

export type AttendanceKind = "clock_in" | "clock_out" | "manual_correction";

export interface AttendanceEventRow {
  id: string; employeeId: string; branchId: string; kind: AttendanceKind;
  occurredAt: string; terminalId: string | null; recordedBy: string;
  note: string | null; correctsEventId: string | null; createdAt: string;
}

export interface RecordAttendanceInput {
  employeeId: string; kind: AttendanceKind; occurredAt: string;
  branchId?: string; terminalId?: string | null; note?: string | null; correctsEventId?: string | null;
}

/**
 * The ONE insert path for attendance — the back-office route and 6A's sync apply both call it.
 * `recorded_by` is omitted from the column list on purpose: the column's DEFAULT plus `stamp_recorded_by`
 * put the acting principal there, and passing a value would be a payload-supplied actor (LEGACY-044).
 * `branchId` defaults to the employee's `home_branch_id`, which is what a back-office correction means.
 *
 * **Returns `null`, and the type says so.** The statement is an `insert … select` over `app.employee`: if
 * the employee is in another company, or is hidden from this actor by `tenant_isolation` or by Form A′ of
 * `branch_isolation` (§4.5.6), the select yields no row, the insert writes nothing and `row` is
 * `undefined`. Both callers — the route in Step 4 and the sync seam in Step 6 — turn that into their own
 * refusal. A `row!` here would type the value as non-nullable while returning `undefined` at run time,
 * which makes the callers' own `if (!created)` guard look like dead code to a reader and to a linter.
 */
export async function recordAttendance(tx: Sql, companyId: string, input: RecordAttendanceInput): Promise<AttendanceEventRow | null> {
  const [row] = await tx<AttendanceEventRow[]>`
    insert into app.attendance_event (tenant_id, company_id, employee_id, branch_id, kind, occurred_at, terminal_id, note, corrects_event_id)
    select app.current_tenant(), ${companyId}, e.id,
           coalesce(${input.branchId ?? null}::uuid, e.home_branch_id),
           ${input.kind}, ${input.occurredAt}::timestamptz, ${input.terminalId ?? null}::uuid,
           ${input.note ?? null}, ${input.correctsEventId ?? null}::uuid
      from app.employee e
     where e.id = ${input.employeeId} and e.company_id = ${companyId}
    returning id, employee_id as "employeeId", branch_id as "branchId", kind, occurred_at as "occurredAt",
              terminal_id as "terminalId", recorded_by as "recordedBy", note,
              corrects_event_id as "correctsEventId", created_at as "createdAt"`;
  return row ?? null;   // zero rows means the employee is not visible; the caller decides what that means
}

export async function listAttendance(
  tx: Sql, companyId: string,
  opts: { limit: number; cursor?: Cursor; employeeId?: string; from?: string; to?: string },
): Promise<{ rows: AttendanceEventRow[]; nextCursor: string | null }> {
  const rows = await tx<AttendanceEventRow[]>`
    select id, employee_id as "employeeId", branch_id as "branchId", kind, occurred_at as "occurredAt",
           terminal_id as "terminalId", recorded_by as "recordedBy", note,
           corrects_event_id as "correctsEventId", created_at as "createdAt"
      from app.attendance_event
     where company_id = ${companyId}
       and (${opts.employeeId ?? null}::uuid is null or employee_id = ${opts.employeeId ?? null}::uuid)
       and (${opts.from ?? null}::timestamptz is null or occurred_at >= ${opts.from ?? null}::timestamptz)
       and (${opts.to ?? null}::timestamptz is null or occurred_at < ${opts.to ?? null}::timestamptz)
       and (${opts.cursor?.id ?? null}::uuid is null or id > ${opts.cursor?.id ?? null}::uuid)
     order by id
     limit ${opts.limit + 1}`;
  const page = rows.slice(0, opts.limit);
  return { rows: page, nextCursor: rows.length > opts.limit ? page[page.length - 1]!.id : null };
}
```

```ts
import { pgSchema, text, timestamp, uuid } from "drizzle-orm/pg-core";

const app = pgSchema("app");

export const attendanceEvent = app.table("attendance_event", {
  id: uuid("id").primaryKey(),
  tenantId: uuid("tenant_id").notNull(),
  companyId: uuid("company_id").notNull(),
  employeeId: uuid("employee_id").notNull(),
  branchId: uuid("branch_id").notNull(),
  kind: text("kind").notNull(),
  occurredAt: timestamp("occurred_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull(),
  terminalId: uuid("terminal_id"),
  recordedBy: uuid("recorded_by").notNull(),
  note: text("note"),
  correctsEventId: uuid("corrects_event_id"),
  createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull(),
});
```

`packages/contracts/src/hr/attendance.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from "@hono/zod-openapi";
import { ListEnvelope, ListQuery, TimestampSchema, UuidSchema } from "../common";

export const AttendanceKindSchema = z.enum(["clock_in", "clock_out", "manual_correction"]);

export const AttendanceEventSchema = z.object({
  id: UuidSchema,
  employeeId: UuidSchema,
  branchId: UuidSchema,
  kind: AttendanceKindSchema,
  occurredAt: TimestampSchema,
  terminalId: UuidSchema.nullable(),
  recordedBy: UuidSchema,
  note: z.string().nullable(),
  correctsEventId: UuidSchema.nullable(),
}).openapi("AttendanceEvent");

/** No `recordedBy`: the actor is the session's (LEGACY-044). No `terminalId` either — a back-office
 *  correction has no till, and the sync path supplies it from the signed call, never from a body. */
export const RecordAttendanceBody = z.object({
  employeeId: UuidSchema,
  kind: AttendanceKindSchema,
  occurredAt: TimestampSchema,
  branchId: UuidSchema.optional(),
  note: z.string().max(500).optional(),
  correctsEventId: UuidSchema.optional(),
}).openapi("RecordAttendanceBody");

export const AttendanceQuery = ListQuery.extend({
  "filter[employeeId][eq]": UuidSchema.optional(),
  "filter[occurredAt][gte]": TimestampSchema.optional(),
  "filter[occurredAt][lt]": TimestampSchema.optional(),
});
export const AttendanceList = ListEnvelope(AttendanceEventSchema);
export type AttendanceEventDto = z.infer<typeof AttendanceEventSchema>;
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the route module**

`apps/api/src/routes/hr/attendance-events.ts` — `POST` gated by `hr.attendance.record`, `GET` by `hr.employee.read`:

```ts
import { createRoute, OpenAPIHono, z } from "@hono/zod-openapi";
import { PERMISSION } from "@dulja/contracts/permissions";
import { AttendanceList, AttendanceQuery, AttendanceEventSchema, RecordAttendanceBody } from "@dulja/contracts/hr/attendance";
import { AppError, ProblemSchema, decodeCursor, encodeCursor } from "@dulja/contracts";
import { listAttendance, recordAttendance } from "@dulja/db";
import type { AppEnv } from "../../context";
import { IdempotencyHeader, type RouteModule } from "../index";
import { requirePermission } from "../../middleware/require-permission";

export const attendanceEventRoutes = new OpenAPIHono<AppEnv>();
const Params = z.object({ companyId: z.string().uuid() });
const scope = { company: "path:companyId", branch: "body:branchId" } as const;
const readScope = { company: "path:companyId" } as const;

attendanceEventRoutes.openapi(
  createRoute({
    method: "get", path: "/", tags: ["hr"],
    request: { params: Params, query: AttendanceQuery },
    middleware: [requirePermission(PERMISSION["hr.employee.read"], readScope)],
    responses: {
      200: { description: "attendance events", content: { "application/json": { schema: AttendanceList } } },
      403: { description: "forbidden", content: { "application/problem+json": { schema: ProblemSchema } } },
    },
  }),
  async (c) => {
    const { companyId } = c.req.valid("param");
    const q = c.req.valid("query");
    const { rows, nextCursor } = await c.var.withTenant((tx) => listAttendance(tx, companyId, {
      limit: q.limit,
      cursor: q.cursor ? decodeCursor(q.cursor) : undefined,
      employeeId: q["filter[employeeId][eq]"],
      from: q["filter[occurredAt][gte]"],
      to: q["filter[occurredAt][lt]"],
    }));
    return c.json({ items: rows, next_cursor: nextCursor ? encodeCursor({ k: [], id: nextCursor }) : null }, 200);
  },
);

attendanceEventRoutes.openapi(
  createRoute({
    method: "post", path: "/", tags: ["hr"],
    request: { params: Params, headers: IdempotencyHeader, body: { content: { "application/json": { schema: RecordAttendanceBody } } } },
    middleware: [requirePermission(PERMISSION["hr.attendance.record"], scope)],
    responses: {
      201: { description: "the recorded event", content: { "application/json": { schema: AttendanceEventSchema } } },
      403: { description: "forbidden", content: { "application/problem+json": { schema: ProblemSchema } } },
      404: { description: "no such employee", content: { "application/problem+json": { schema: ProblemSchema } } },
    },
  }),
  async (c) => {
    const { companyId } = c.req.valid("param");
    const body = c.req.valid("json");
    const row = await c.var.withTenant(async (tx) => {
      const created = await recordAttendance(tx, companyId, { ...body, terminalId: null });
      if (!created) throw new AppError("ERR_NOT_FOUND", `employee ${body.employeeId} is not visible in company ${companyId}`);
      return created;
    });
    return c.json(row, 201);
  },
);

export const attendanceEventRouteModule: RouteModule = {
  path: "/v1/companies/:companyId/attendance-events",
  router: attendanceEventRoutes,
  permissions: [PERMISSION["hr.employee.read"], PERMISSION["hr.attendance.record"]],
  auth: "human",
};
```

Register it in `buildRoutes` and add its `PROBES` entry (seed an employee and one event for the owner tenant; `expect: 403` on the attacker's `GET`).

```ts
  it("records an event attributed to the employee and acted by the caller", async () => {
    const res = await api.request({ method: "POST", path: `/v1/companies/${s.company}/attendance-events`, as: s.human,
      body: { employeeId: employee, kind: "clock_in", occurredAt: "2026-01-15T01:00:00Z" } });
    expect(res.status).toBe(201);
    const body = await res.json() as { employeeId: string; recordedBy: string; branchId: string; terminalId: string | null };
    expect(body).toMatchObject({ employeeId: employee, recordedBy: s.human, terminalId: null });
    expect(body.branchId).toBe(branchA);          // omitted from the body: it defaults to home_branch_id
  });

  it("an employee of another tenant is 404, never 403", async () => {
    const other = await seedTenant(api.db, "att-other");
    const foreign = await seedEmployee(api.db, other, { employeeNumber: "E-X" });
    const res = await api.request({ method: "POST", path: `/v1/companies/${s.company}/attendance-events`, as: s.human,
      body: { employeeId: foreign, kind: "clock_in", occurredAt: "2026-01-15T01:00:00Z" } });
    expect(res.status).toBe(404);
    expect((await problemOf(res)).code).toBe("ERR_NOT_FOUND");
  });

  it("a reader may list and may not record — the whole point of two codes on one module", async () => {
    // `branchActor` is T9's helper and does not exist yet; a second seed with one code says the same thing.

    await grantCodes(api.db, reader, { codes: ["hr.employee.read"] });
    await api.loadPrincipals();
    const list = await api.request({ method: "GET", path: `/v1/companies/${reader.company}/attendance-events`, as: reader.human });
    expect(list.status).toBe(200);
    const write = await api.request({ method: "POST", path: `/v1/companies/${reader.company}/attendance-events`, as: reader.human,
      body: { employeeId: employee, kind: "clock_in", occurredAt: "2026-01-15T01:00:00Z" } });
    expect(write.status).toBe(403);
    expect((await problemOf(write)).code).toBe("ERR_RBAC_FORBIDDEN");
  });

  it("the list honours both declared filters and returns the standard envelope", async () => {
    const res = await api.request({ method: "GET", as: s.human,
      path: `/v1/companies/${s.company}/attendance-events?filter[employeeId][eq]=${employee}&filter[occurredAt][gte]=2026-01-15T00:00:00Z` });
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    const body = await res.json() as { items: { employeeId: string }[]; next_cursor: string | null };
    expect(body.items.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    for (const row of body.items) expect(row.employeeId).toBe(employee);
    expect(body).toHaveProperty("next_cursor");
  });

  it("a POST with no Idempotency-Key is 400 (spec:2055)", async () => {
    const res = await api.request({ method: "POST", path: `/v1/companies/${s.company}/attendance-events`, as: s.human,
      body: { employeeId: employee, kind: "clock_out", occurredAt: "2026-01-15T09:00:00Z" }, idempotencyKey: null });
    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
    expect((await problemOf(res)).code).toBe("ERR_IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_MISSING");

});
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Replace 6A's attendance refusal with the insert — in THIS task, not Task 9 (amendment A8)**

**The refusal dies the moment this task's migration is applied, and the insert has to land in the same PR.** 6A's `applyBatch` step 6 refuses a ticket carrying a non-empty `attendance` array **behind `to_regclass('app.attendance_event') is null`** (6A:3679), with 6C's first task named as the replacement. `startDb()` applies every discovered migration, so as soon as 0067 exists the guard evaluates false, 6A's code has **no insert path**, and a ticket carrying a clock-in is accepted with the HR event silently dropped — the loss spec:1305 forbids and the exact failure 6A called "loud on purpose". Leaving it to Task 9 would also leave 6A's `packages/db/test/sync/apply.test.ts` red across Tasks 4–8, against Global Constraint 17.

`recordAttendance` (Step 3) is written in this task, so nothing is missing. Inside 6A's per-ticket savepoint, **delete** the `to_regclass('app.attendance_event') is null` refusal and put the insert where it stood — after the lines are inserted and before `postDocument`:

```ts
      // ── 6C: the attendance path (amendment A8) — replaces 6A's ERR_SYNC_MESSAGE_UNSUPPORTED refusal.
      // The clock feed rides on the ticket (§4.10.1) and is inserted against the SAME savepoint: a ticket
      // that is refused for any later reason takes its clock-in back with it, which is what makes the till's
      // outbox row still authoritative. `terminal` is applyBatch's THIRD parameter (TerminalApplyContext);
      // `ctx` is the TenantContext and carries neither a branch nor a terminal.
      for (const a of ticket.attendance) {
        const [row] = await sp<{ employeeId: string }[]>`
          select employee_id as "employeeId" from app.pos_cashier
           where principal_id = ${a.principalId} and company_id = ${terminal.companyId}
             and branch_id = ${terminal.branchId} and revoked_at is null`;
        if (!row) throw new SyncError("ERR_NOT_FOUND", `no active cashier ${a.principalId} at branch ${terminal.branchId}`);
        const event = await recordAttendance(sp, terminal.companyId, {
          employeeId: row.employeeId, kind: a.kind as AttendanceKind, occurredAt: a.at,
          branchId: terminal.branchId, terminalId: terminal.terminalId,
          // recorded_by is NOT passed: the column's stamp trigger writes the TERMINAL, which is the acting
          // principal on this path (hazard 19). `pos.attendance.clock` is evaluated at the till from the
          // snapshot; 6B adds the server-side principal_has line in the PR that seeds the code (A10).
        });
        // `recordAttendance` answers null when its `insert … select` matched no employee. It cannot happen
        // on this path — the `pos_cashier` row above proves the employee exists in this company — but a
        // dropped clock-in is exactly the silent loss spec:1305 forbids, so the impossible case REFUSES the
        // ticket instead of returning `accepted` with nothing written. The till keeps its outbox row.
        if (!event) throw new SyncError("ERR_NOT_FOUND", `employee ${row.employeeId} is not visible to this terminal`);
      }
```

`SyncError` and `ERR_NOT_FOUND` are both 6A's (`packages/db/src/sync/enrolment.ts`; `ERR_NOT_FOUND` is already a `SyncErrorCode` member), so this step widens nothing and imports no `AppError` — `dependency-rule` pins `packages/db` to `['@dulja/domain']`.

**Then update 6A's own case in the same commit.** `packages/db/test/sync/apply.test.ts` asserts `ERR_SYNC_MESSAGE_UNSUPPORTED` for a ticket carrying `attendance`. That assertion is now wrong, and this task replaces it — word for word the handover §4.10.1's last paragraph writes for 6D and `shifts`. Change the case to assert the stored row and leave every other clause of 6A's test untouched:

```ts
// clock-in's employee through it, and 6C's verifyTicket refuses a ticket whose cashier has none.
  const cashierEmployee = await seedEmployee(api.db, s, { employeeNumber: `${name}-CASHIER` });
```
